A wide mantle fringes the sides of the mountain. In the distance you can see
other peaks as tall as the one you are on surrounded by a blur of rich green.
Thick fog obstructs the view to the east. It seems to reach down out of the sky

hillside.

greater werewolf (stunned) and a werebear. A werewolf pelt, a werewolf tooth, a
werewolf pelt, a werewolf pelt and a werewolf claw are here.

[Darsett Commons]
Simply a cleared out area surrounded by a few scattered huts, this seems to be
the meeting area for the hamlet of Darsett. A fire pit in the center lays cold
and unused, its ashes scattered in the winds. A trail leads back down the
hillside.
You see Stolar (laying), Sy, Khalil, Young (dead), Sun, Feras (dead), Cypress,
Mariella, Bethesdan, Kylie, Rion, a greater werewolf (imprisoned), a lesser
werewolf (dead), a greater werewolf (imprisoned), a greater werewolf (dead), a
